## the drivers' BREAK98 scenarios (the rest as in results/lossygate/g1_chain_sim_2026-11-17.txt and t1c_tue_follower3_sim_2026-11-16.txt)
bm() { mkdir -p "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98"; echo "BREAK98 test X (stub), expected end $(t 600)" > "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98/BREAK98_HOLD"; }
bmoff() { rm -f "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98/BREAK98_HOLD"; }
b1() { setup b1; bm b1; run b1; gates_on b1 g1r; waitlog b1 "held: BREAK98_HOLD" 60; sleep 8
  note b1 "the marker removed; a runner call before: $(test -s $D/b1/runner_calls.txt && echo YES || echo no)"; bmoff b1
  wait $CPID; report b1 $? "BREAK98_HOLD at the start: held, no start; removed: start, done"; }
b2() { setup b2; run b2 STUB_S_lx45g1b=30; gates_on b2 g1r; waitrun b2 "tg=lx45g1b tier=1 resume=0"; note b2 "B1's runner started; BREAK98_HOLD appears"; bm b2
  waitlog b2 "B1 base forward yields" 60; note b2 "the yield is logged"; sleep 5; note b2 "the marker removed"; bmoff b2
  wait $CPID; report b2 $? "BREAK98_HOLD mid-B1: the runner stopped at once (one read), peer unlock; held while it exists; removed: B1 resumes (CARD_RESUME=1); done"; }
b3() { setup b3; run b3 STUB_S_g1r45g1b=30; gates_on b3 g1r; waitrun b3 "tg=g1r45g1b tier=0 resume=0"; note b3 "V0 started; BREAK98_HOLD appears"; bm b3
  waitlog b3 "V0 g1r tier 0 vs B0 yields" 60; sleep 4; note b3 "the marker removed"; bmoff b3
  wait $CPID; report b3 $? "BREAK98_HOLD mid-V0 (tier 0): yield; the cut run moved aside (.cut1), V0 fresh; done"; }
b4() { setup b4; local DC; DC=$(tc 1020); note b4 "day cutoff $DC"; run b4 CHAIN_CUTOFF="$DC" STUB_S_lx45g1b=30; gates_on b4 g1r
  waitrun b4 "tg=lx45g1b tier=1 resume=0"; note b4 "B1 started; BREAK98_HOLD appears and stays"; bm b4
  waitlog b4 "the day window cut G1" 400; sleep 3; note b4 "the marker removed; the night marker"; bmoff b4; night_on b4
  wait $CPID; report b4 $? "BREAK98_HOLD mid-B1 past the day's last start: B1 rc 75; the night window on its marker; B1 resumes; done"; }
bm() { mkdir -p "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98"; echo "BREAK98 test X (stub), expected end $(t 600)" > "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98/BREAK98_HOLD"; }
bmoff() { rm -f "$D/$1/NewIdeas/research/week98/BREAK98_HOLD"; }
h1() { setup h1; bm h1; run h1; waitlog h1 "waiting: BREAK98_HOLD" 60; sleep 3
  note h1 "the marker removed; a chain launch before this: $(grep -q 'runs as pid' "$(LOGF h1)" && echo YES || echo no)"; bmoff h1
  wait $FPID; report h1 $? "BREAK98_HOLD at the start gate: no start; removed: start, done"; }
h2() { setup h2; run h2 STUB_B2=300; waitrun h2 "tag=lx45t1c1_rev resume=0"; note h2 "B2's runner started; BREAK98_HOLD appears"; bm h2
  waitlog h2 "B2 base reverse yields" 60; note h2 "the yield is logged"; sleep 5; note h2 "the marker removed"; bmoff h2
  wait $FPID; report h2 $? "BREAK98_HOLD mid-B2: chain2 stops the runner at once (peer unlock); held; removed: B2 resumes (CARD_RESUME=1), V2; done"; }
h3() { setup h3; local DC; DC=$(t 1080); note h3 "day cutoff $DC"; run h3 STUB_B2=600 CHAIN_DAYCUT="$DC"
  waitrun h3 "tag=lx45t1c1_rev resume=0"; note h3 "B2's runner started; BREAK98_HOLD appears and stays"; bm h3
  wait $FPID; report h3 $? "BREAK98_HOLD mid-B2 that stays until no start is left: B2 rc 75; the day route ends without V2 (leftovers to Wednesday)"; }
h4() { setup h4; run h4; waitlog h4 "runs as pid" 60; bm h4; note h4 "BREAK98_HOLD right after the chain's launch"
  sleep 25; note h4 "the marker removed; runner calls before: $(test -s $D/h4/runner_calls.txt && echo YES || echo none)"; bmoff h4
  wait $FPID; report h4 $? "BREAK98_HOLD right after chain2's launch, before its runner: not started (held); removed: B2, V2; done"; }
ALL="a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 h1 h2 h3 h4"
